Negative means before event, positive after. \n  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n\n## Python environment\n\n- All required Python packages are already installed in the system Python environment.\n- Use `python3` to run any python code.\n- If you need to install additional libraries, use `pip install`.\n\n## Decoder Reference\n\n- Run this script with `python train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n- Optional arguments:\n  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n\n### Purpose\n\nThese functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n1. requirement.\n2. **Merge probes within a session**: Reference code merges probes for a session before decoding; we will do the same.\n3. **Filter to valid trials explicitly**: Use trial masks / valid reference practice of using cluster labels/QC and preserve metadata for sanity checks.\n5. **Represent per-trial categorical outputs compactly**: Choice and prior can be stored as per-trial vectors; Although a session may have included multiple probe insertions, we did not perform decoding on these probes\r  separately because they are not independent. Instead, neurons in the same session and region were combined across probes for our decoding analysis. Decoding wa\r s cross-validated and compared with a null distribution to test for significance. A given region may have been recorded on multiple sessions; therefore, in the\r main figures (Figs. 4–7) the region P value was defined by combining session P values using Fisher’s combined probability test, and the region effect size was d\r efined by subtracting the median of the null distribution from the decoding score and reporting the median of the resulting values across sessions. N_BINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   149\t        lo = np.searchsorted(spike_times, edges[0], side='left')\n   150\t        hi = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t0 + T_END, side='left')\n   151\t        ts = spike_times[lo:hi] - t0\n   152\t        cl = spike_clusters[lo:hi]\n   153\t        bins = np.floor((ts - T_START) / BINSIZE).astype(int)\n   154\t        m = (bins >= 0) & (bins < N_BINS) & (cl >= 170\t    position = np.asarray(position, dtype=float)\n   171\t    if len(timestamps) < 2 or len(position) < 2:\n   172\t        return [np.full(N_BINS, np.nan, dtype=np.float32) for _ in stim_on]\n   173\t    keep = np.isfinite(timestamps) & np.isfinite(position)\n   174\t    timestamps = timestamps[keep]\n   175\t    position = position[keep]\n   176\t    if len(timestamps) < 2:\n   177\t 184\t    trials = []\n   185\t    for t0 in stim_on:\n   186\t        x = t0 + TIME_CENTERS\n   187\t        y = np.interp(x, timestamps, vel, left=np.nan, right=np.nan)\n   188\t np.load(mp).astype(np.float32)))\n   201\t    if not streams:\n   202\t        return None\n   203\t    return streams\n   204\t\n   205\t\n   206\tdef interp_motion_energy(streams, stim_on):\n   207\t    trials = []\n   208\t    for t0 in stim_on:\n   209\t        x = t0 + TIME_CENTERS\n   210\t        ys = []\n   211\t        for ts, me in streams:\n   212\t            ys.append(np.interp(x, trials.append(y.astype(np.float32))\n   219\t    return trials\n   220\t\n   221\t\n   222\tdef discretize_tertiles(list_of_arrays):\n   223\t    allv = np.concatenate([x[np.isfinite(x)] for x in list_of_arrays if np.isfinite(x).any()])\n   224\t    q1, q2 = np.quantile(allv, [1/3, 2/3]) if len(allv) else (0.0, 1.0)\n   225\t    out = []\n   226\t    for x in list_of_arrays:\n   227\t        y
